      "content": "        raw_counts=np.array(stats[\"raw_counts\"]),\n    )\n\n    print(f\"  Saved: {jsonl_path}\")\n    print(f\"  Saved: {npz_path}\")\n    return jsonl_path, npz_path\n\n\n# ── Main ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef run_single(dataset_name, model_key, prompt_type, sample_size, split, api_key):\n    \"\"\"Run annotation for one (dataset, model, prompt) combination.\"\"\"\n    config = DATASET_CONFIGS[dataset_name]\n    model_id = MODELS[model_key]\n\n    output_dir = RESULTS_DIR / dataset_name\n    output_path = output_dir / f\"{model_key}_{prompt_type}.jsonl\"\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Dataset: {dataset_name} | Model: {model_key} ({model_id})\")\n    print(f\"Prompt: {prompt_type} | Sample: {sample_size} | Split: {split}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n    samples = load_data(dataset_name, split, sample_size)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(samples)} samples\")\n\n    # Resume support\n    existing = load_existing_results(output_path)\n    if existing:\n        remaining = [s for s in samples if s[\"idx\"] not in existing]\n        print(f\"Resuming: {len(existing)} done, {len(remaining)} remaining\")\n    else:\n        remaining = samples\n\n    if not remaining:\n        print(\"All samples already annotated. Recomputing confusion matrix...\")\n        all_results = [existing[s[\"idx\"]] for s in samples if s[\"idx\"] in existing]\n    else:\n        pipeline = AnnotationPipeline(API_BASE, api_key, model_id)\n        new_results = pipeline.annotate_batch(remaining, config, prompt_type, output_dir=output_dir)\n\n        merged = dict(existing)\n        for r in new_results:\n            merged[r[\"idx\"]] = r\n        all_results = [merged[s[\"idx\"]] for s in samples if s[\"idx\"] in merged]\n\n    # Compute confusion matrix\n    gold = [r[\"gold_label\"] for r in all_results]\n    pred = [r[\"pred_label\"] for r in all_results]\n    C, class_to_idx, stats = compute_confusion_matrix(gold, pred, config[\"classes\"])\n\n    raw_counts = np.array(stats[\"raw_counts\"])\n    col_counts = raw_counts.sum(axis=0).astype(int)\n    stats[\"per_column_counts\"] = {cls: int(col_counts[i]) for i, cls in enumerate(config[\"classes\"])}\n\n    if dataset_name == \"humaid\":\n        low_cols = [(cls, int(col_counts[i])) for i, cls in enumerate(config[\"classes\"]) if col_counts[i] < 20]\n        if low_cols:\n            print(f\"\\n  WARNING: HumAID columns with < 20 samples:\")\n            for cls, cnt in low_cols:\n                print(f\"    {cls}: {cnt}\")\n            stats[\"low_column_warning\"] = [{\"class\": cls, \"count\": cnt} for cls, cnt in low_cols]\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Per-column sample counts:\")\n    for cls in config[\"classes\"]:\n        print(f\"    {cls}: {stats['per_column_counts'][cls]}\")\n\n    save_results(all_results, C, class_to_idx, stats, output_dir, model_key, prompt_type)\n\n    n_parsed = sum(1 for r in all_results if r[\"pred_label\"] is not None)\n    n_correct = sum(1 for r in all_results if r[\"pred_label\"] == r[\"gold_label\"])\n    print(f\"\\nResults: {n_parsed}/{len(all_results)} parsed, \"\n          f\"{n_correct}/{n_parsed} correct ({n_correct/max(n_parsed,1)*100:.1f}%)\")\n    print(f\"Confusion matrix coverage: {stats['coverage']:.1%}\")\n    print(f\"Confusion matrix C (column-stochastic):\\n{np.array2string(C, precision=3)}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"LLM Annotation Pipeline for Causal Annotation Error Attribution\"",

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：plan011 frontier annotations\n\n在 home-1x5090 服务器上执行 frontier LLM 标注任务。\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 主机：home-1x5090（SSH alias）\n- 账户：sonny\n- 项目目录：`/home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/`\n- 标注脚本：`/home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/annotate.py`\n- 结果目录：`/home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/results/`\n- API endpoint：`http://47.94.22.126/v1`\n\n### API Key\n```\nOPENROUTER_API_KEY=[REDACTED]\n```\n\n### 任务清单（按优先级）\n\n**Step 0: 验证连通性**\nSSH 到服务器，先测试 API 是否可达：\n```bash\ncurl -s -w \"\\nHTTP: %{http_code}\\n\" --connect-timeout 5 \"http://47.94.22.126/v1/models\" -H \"Authorization: [REDACTED]\" | head -5\n```\n如果不通，立即报告并停止。\n\n**Step 1: 同步最新代码**\n```bash\ncd /home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib && git pull\n```\n\n**Step 2: 检查现有数据**\n```bash\nls -la /home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/results/vast/\nls -la /home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/results/civil_comments/\nls -la /home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/results/humaid/qwen3_few-shot-5*\n```\n记录哪些已完成、哪些需要运行。\n\n**Step 3: 恢复 HumAID qwen3_fs5**\n上次因 402 全部失败（0/1500 valid）。用新 key 恢复：\n```bash\ncd /home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts\nexport OPENROUTER_API_KEY=[REDACTED]\npython3 annotate.py --dataset humaid --model qwen3 --prompt few-shot-5 --sample 1500\n```\n脚本有 resume 支持（streaming write），402 的条目会被重跑。\n\n**Step 4: VAST (K=3) frontier annotations**\n3 models × 3 prompts = 9 runs：\n```bash\nfor model in llama4 qwen3 deepseek; do\n    for prompt in zero-shot few-shot-3 few-shot-5; do\n        echo \"=== Running vast / $model / $prompt ===\"\n        python3 annotate.py --dataset vast --model $model --prompt $prompt --sample 500\n    done\ndone\n```\n脚本有 resume 支持，已完成的会跳过。\n\n**Step 5: CivilComments (K=2) frontier annotations**\n3 models × 3 prompts = 9 runs：\n```bash\nfor model in llama4 qwen3 deepseek; do\n    for prompt in zero-shot few-shot-3 few-shot-5; do\n        echo \"=== Running civil_comments / $model / $prompt ===\"\n        python3 annotate.py --dataset civil_comments --model $model --prompt $prompt --sample 500\n    done\ndone\n```\n\n**Step 6: 验证结果**\n完成后列出所有生成的文件和 confusion matrix：\n```bash\nfind /home/sonny/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/results/ -name \"*.npz\" -newer /tmp/plan011_start | sort\n```\n对每个新生成的 .npz 文件，用 python 加载并打印 confusion matrix 对角线均值：\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nimport glob\nfor f in sorted(glob.glob(\"results/*/*.npz\")):\n    d = np.load(f, allow_pickle=True)\n    C = d[\"confusion_matrix\"]\n    print(f\"{f}: diag_mean={np.diag(C).mean():.3f}, acc={np.trace(C)/C.sum():.3f}\")\n```\n\n**Step 7: 汇总报告**\n汇总所有结果到一个表格，包括：\n- dataset, model, prompt, accuracy, diag_mean, parse_rate\n- 标注 OK/WARN/FAIL\n\n### 注意事项\n- 每个 API 调用间有 0.5s delay（脚本默认）\n- 如果遇到 402，说明额度又用完了，立即停止并报告已完成的部分\n- 如果遇到 rate limit (429)，脚本有自动重试\n- 先跑一个小测试（如 vast/llama4/zero-shot）验证 API 正常，再批量执行\n- 注意：vast 数据集需要 topic_field，脚本已支持\n\n### 输出\n完成后 update_registry 更新 plan003 的 metrics（qwen3_fs5 恢复结果），并创建 plan011 registry entry 记录 VAST+CivilComments frontier 结果。\n"

Collider \"protective\" vs Violation=100% 矛盾澄清** (`experiments.tex`)\n- 在 structural guarantee 段落中添加：100% violation/amplification 是相对于 unconditional τ 的，而 \"protective\" 指 |B(C)| 随误分类严重度单调递减——conditioning on noisy A* 比 conditioning on perfect A 产生更少的 collider bias（引用 Conjecture 1）\n\n**2b. Cross-PPI gold-free 讨论深化** (`experiments.tex`)\n- 展开解释 Cross-PPI 机制：用两个独立 LLM annotator 互为参考，在 annotator-independence 假设下提供 valid confidence intervals。关键点：因为 Cross-PPI 作用于 estimator output 而非直接操作 C 矩阵，不受 FWL invariance 限制，理论上可校正全部 7 种拓扑\n\n**2c. K≥3 novelty 论证强化** (`introduction.tex`)\n- 重写为 \"qualitative break, not gradual degradation\"：K=2 的系数符号保持是 2×2 矩阵维度的代数性质（非特定 error rate），K≥3 该维度约束消失，即使对角 >0.5 也可能 sign reversal\n\n### Task 1: plan011 Frontier Annotations 🔄 进行中\n\n- API key 已获取并测试（从本机不可达，需从 home-1x5090 执行）\n- Worker `plan011_frontier_annotations` 已 spawn，正在 SSH 到 home-1x5090 执行：\n  - 恢复 HumAID qwen3_fs5（之前因 402 全部失败）\n  - VAST (K=3): 3 models × 3 prompts = 9 runs\n  - CivilComments (K=2): 3 models × 3 prompts = 9 runs\n\n### Task 3: 论文数据更新 ⏳ 等待 Task 1 完成",

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

## Summary Report: Data Files and Empirical Validation Scripts

### Data Files (.npz and .jsonl)

**Found in results directories:**

1. **JSONL Files:**
   - `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/plan011_frontier_annotations/data/civil_comments/test.jsonl` (357K)
   - `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/plan011_frontier_annotations/data/vast/test.jsonl` (679K)

2. **NPZ Files:**
   - None currently present in the results directories. However, `compute_results.py` in plan011 is designed to generate `.npz` files when executed. Expected outputs would be:
     - `results/civil_comments/{model}_zero-shot_confusion_matrix.npz`
     - `results/vast/{model}_zero-shot_confusion_matrix.npz`

### JSON Result Files

**Primary empirical validation and results files:**

1. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/ppi_empirical_validation.json` (72K)
   - Output from `compute_ppi_empirical.py`
   - Contains empirical bias reduction metrics across 7 topologies × 5 correction methods
   - Validates PPI++, naive plug-in, DSL, MC-SIMEX, and MLA corrections

2. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/theory_empirical_mape.json` (47K)
   - Output from `compute_theory_empirical_mape.py`
   - Compares analytical plim bias (theory) against MC empirical bias
   - Scope: 56 scenarios (8 HumAID configs × 7 topologies), K=10 only

3. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/delta_max_results.json` (23K)
   - Output from `compute_delta_max.py`
   - Computes delta_max constraints for all confusion matrices
   - Verifies ASV validity across confusion matrices

4. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/plan006/asv_empirical_results.json` (119K)
   - Contains 8 valid HumAID K=10 confusion matrices
   - F1 vs bias analysis
   - Input for PPI empirical validation

### Primary Python Scripts for Empirical Validation & Bias Computation

**Core empirical validation scripts:**

1. **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/compute_ppi_empirical.py`** (22K)
   - Loads HumAID confusion matrices
   - Generates synthetic MC data using plan_006's K=10 DGP for 7 topologies
   - Runs all 5 correction methods (PPI++, naive plug-in, DSL, MC-SIMEX, MLA)
   - Reports empirical bias reduction per topology × method
   - Key functions: `run_single_rep()`, `compute_tau_true()`, `ppi_wald()`, `mcsimex_wald()`

2. **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/compute_theory_empirical_mape.py`** (8K)
   - Compares analytical plim bias against MC empirical bias
   - Computes MAPE (Mean Absolute Percentage Error)
   - Key functions: `mc_tau_hat()`, `run_mc_block()`
   - Imports from plan_006 for DGP and plim computation

3. **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/compute_delta_max.py`** (18K)
   - Computes and validates delta_max constraints
   - Loads confusion matrices from HumAID (K=10), VAST (K=3), CivilComments (K=2)
   - Verifies all reported ASV values are within valid ranges
   - Uses `annot_sensitivity.confusion.delta_range()` for validation

**Plan-specific empirical computation scripts:**

4. **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/plan011_frontier_annotations/scripts/compute_results.py`** (12K)
   - Processes frontier-model annotations from JSONL
   - Builds confusion matrices for VAST (K=3) and CivilComments (K=2)
   - Computes per-topology biases and ASVs
   - Outputs `.npz` and `.json` confusion matrices
   - Generates `frontier_results.json` with F1, bias, and ASV metrics

5. **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/plan012_case_study/case_study_analysis.py`**
   - Case study analysis for specific confusion matrices

6. **`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/compute_all_tasks.py`** (33K)
   - Orchestrates all computation tasks across plans
   - Coordinates execution of ASV empirical analysis, corrections, and validations

**Related utility/validation scripts:**

7. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/annotate.py` (25K)
   - Annotation pipeline for generating gold-standard labels
   - Input processing for confusion matrix construction

8. `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod_0510/projects/causal_annot_error_attrib/artifacts/compute_theory_empirical_mape.py` - Handles theory-to-empirical validation

All these scripts import from the core `annot_sensitivity` package (local) which provides correction methods, confusion matrix utilities, and DGP infrastructure.
